Have a curved inner surface at radius cradle_inner_radius ≈ 10.4 mm from pole axis\n    # 2. Extend to radius cradle_outer_radius ≈ 11.6 mm\n    # 3. Connect to the base\n\n    # Create a support structure: a curved wedge from base to pole axis height\n    # Using simple geometry: create vertical supports at the sides that connect to the base\n    # and angle toward the pole\n\n    # Support ribs on left and right\n    rib_thickness = backing_thickness + 1.0\n\n    # Left rib - semicircular in cross-section\n        # The semicircle has center at (0, pole_axis_height) and radius cradle_outer_radius\n        # Draw the semicircle from the base up to cradle the pole\n\n        # Manually draw the cradle profile\n        # Semicircle from -cradle_outer_radius to +cradle_outer_radius (bottom half)\n        # Center at (0, pole_axis_height)\n\n        with Locations((0, pole_axis_height)):\n            # Create a semicircular arc\n            # Draw from left (-cradle_outer_radius, 0) to right (+cradle_outer_radius, 0)\n            # with the curve going down (into negative Z)\n            # Then close with vertical lines on each side\n\n            # Use JoinType to create a profile\n            points = [\n                (-half_width, base_height_z),  # left base\n                (-half_width, pole_axis_height - cradle_outer_radius),  # left vertical\n            ]\n\n            # Add semicircle points (bottom half)\n            num_arc_points = 10\n            for i in range(num_arc_points + 1):\n                angle = pi - (i / num_arc_points) * pi  # from 180 to 0 degrees\n                x = cradle_outer_radius * cos(angle)\n                z = cradle_outer_radius * sin(angle)\n                if i > 0:  # skip first point to avoid duplication\n                    points.append((x, pole_axis_height + z))\n\n            points.extend([\n                (half_width, pole_axis_height - cradle_outer_radius),  # right vertical\n                (half_width, base_height_z),  # right base\n                (-half_width, base_height_z),  # close the profile\n            ])\n\n            # Create a polyline from the points\n            # But BuildSketch might not support this directly\n            # Let me use a different approach\n\n    # Use a simpler approach: create base, then use extrude with Path or profile\n    # Actually, let me just create the geometry procedurally\n\n    # Create base\n    base = Box(base_width_x, base_length_y, base_height_z)\n    base = base.translate((0, 0, base_height_z / 2))\n\n    # Create the cradle by combining base with a half-cylinder support structure\n    # The cradle should:\n    # 1. Have a curved inner surface at radius cradle_inner_radius ≈ 10.4 mm from pole axis\n    # 2. Extend to radius cradle_outer_radius ≈ 11.6 mm\n    # 3. Connect to the base\n\n    # Create a support structure: a curved wedge from base to pole axis height\n    # Using simple geometry: create vertical supports at the sides that connect to the base\n    # and angle toward the pole\n\n    # Support ribs on left and right\n    rib_thickness = backing_thickness + 1.0\n\n    # Left rib - angled support\n    left_rib = Box(rib_thickness, base_length_y, pole_axis_height - base_height_z - cradle_outer_radius)\n    left_rib = left_rib.translate(\n        (-half_width + rib_thickness / 2, 0, base_height_z + (pole_axis_height - base_height_z - cradle_outer_radius) / 2)\n    )\n\n    # Right rib - symmetric\n    right_rib = Box(rib_thickness, base_length_y, pole_axis_height - base_height_z - cradle_outer_radius)\n    right_rib = right_rib.translate(\n        (half_width - rib_thickness / 2, 0, base_height_z + (pole_axis_height - base_height_z - cradle_outer_radius) / 2)\n    )\n\n    # Create the cradle curved surface as a half-cylinder\n    half_cyl = Cylinder(radius=cradle_outer_radius, height=base_length_y)\n    half_cyl = half_cyl.rotate(Axis.X, 90)\n    half_cyl = half_cyl.translate((0, 0, pole_axis_height))\n\n    # Cut off the top half\n    cutter = Box(2 * cradle_outer_radius + 2, base_length_y + 2, cradle_outer_radius + 1)\n    cutter = cutter.translate((0, 0, pole_axis_height))\n    cradle_half = half_cyl - cutter\n\n    # Remove the inner void to create clearance\n    inner_cyl = Cylinder(radius=cradle_inner_radius, height=base_length_y)\n    inner_cyl = inner_cyl.rotate(Axis.X, 90)\n    inner_cyl = inner_cyl.translate((0, 0, pole_axis_height))\n\n    # Final geometry: base + ribs + cradle - inner void\n    result = base + left_rib + right_rib + cradle_half - inner_cyl\n\n    # Polish edges\n    edges_to_polish = result.edges()\n    result = polish(result, edges_to_polish, 1.0)\n\n    return result\n","userModified":false}}
